Conclusion: 以分隔符字符串标记聊天角色/轮次存在根本性伪造风险，控制字符串与标识符的分离是提升安全性的有效途径。无名分词机制可解决这一问题，并在实际攻击场景下显著提升安全性和识别率。

Abstract: Open-weight language models publish the strings their chat templates use to mark turns, roles and tool results, which the tokenizer maps back to the reserved identifiers the model obeys. Anyone who controls text in a prompt can therefore write a turn boundary indistinguishable from one the serving stack wrote. We audit 256 deployed chat tokenizers. All are forgeable, and the flag usually recommended as a fix leaves 56.6% forgeable because it misses the tool and reasoning markers agent systems rely on. We propose nameless tokenization, which leaves the control entries with a reserved identifier and no surface string, so the content encoder cannot emit one and message content reaches the model unaltered. Across five tokenizer families it reproduces the standard token stream exactly on attack-free data and lifts accuracy on a probe of delimiter-bearing text from 8.5% to 59.9%, where sanitizers lose it. Separating a delimiter's appearance from its identifier shows the identifier matters little against a bare task instruction, but carries most of a forged tool result and most of any forged turn once the system message tells the model to treat user content as data.

</details>


### [148] [Autoformalizing Argumentative Material Inferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16991)
*Xin Quan,Reto Gubelmann,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GUARD，一个神经-符号混合的自动形式化框架，实现了将自然语言论证材料可靠转化为可机检的形式推理，显著提升了论证自动验证的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自然语言论证在逻辑形式化过程中，很多前提、假设和例外条件往往隐含于文本，对形式验证与机器检验非常不利。直接将自然语言翻译为逻辑表达式难以保证推理的忠实与严格。

Method: 作者提出以‘guard completion（护卫补全）’的新方法，将隐式的非单调推理转化为显式的单调形式推理，需构建显式前提集合。具体实现为：利用大模型（LLMs）提出并形式化补全前提候选，Isabelle/HOL进行定理验证与反馈，若无法形成忠实的形式化结果则选择放弃。

Result: 在Debatepedia和ARCT数据集上，用不同的LLM实验。GUARD在可验证的忠实性上提升了35.3/32.9个百分点，推理泄漏率降低25.9/21.9个百分点，明显优于最新的基于LLM的自动定理证明方法。

Conclusion: 神经-符号协作并结合显式假设层和‘软批判’机制可以显著提升论证材料的自动形式化和机检推理精度，提高了论证自动验证的可靠性与有效性。

Abstract: Natural language arguments are compelling before they are formally explicit. A premise supports a claim through defeasible warrants, background commitments, and exception conditions that the text leaves implicit. However, formal verification requires the opposite. Making such arguments machine-checkable requires constructing the missing commitments, not only translating given sentences into logic. Construction, however, carries a risk that translation does not: a system free to add premises can make any claim provable, and a formally valid proof may assert the claim outright, prove it without the original premise, or establish more than the claim itself. We address this problem by formulating autoformalization for argumentative material inference as guard completion, in which non-monotonic material support is turned into monotonic formal inference relative to an explicitly constructed guard set. A completion is accepted only when its proof both passes the theorem prover and survives contrastive tests of premise dependence and claim selectivity. We implement this formulation in GUARD, a neuro-symbolic framework in which LLMs construct and formalize candidate guards, Isabelle/HOL verifies the resulting theories and returns step-level feedback for iterative refinement, and the system abstains when no faithful completion can be reached. Our empirical results on Debatepedia and ARCT using different LLMs demonstrate that GUARD yields significant improvements in verified-faithful (+35.3, +32.9 points) and substantial reductions in leakage (-25.9, -21.9 points) over the state-of-the-art LLM-driven theorem proving approach. Moreover, we show that the symbolic soft critique and the explicit assumption layer account for most of these gains, with the soft critique also improving the initial validity of the elicited context and reducing the number of iterations required for successful verification.

</details>


### [149] [The Role of Implicit and Explicit Demographic Signals in Large Language Model-based Student Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16993)
*Donya Rooein,Luca Benedetto,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文研究了大型语言模型在学生评估任务中对学生人口统计学特征（如教育水平、社会经济背景等）的敏感性，通过设置显性和隐性人口特征，发现这些模型会根据人口信息调整评分和反馈，甚至在隐性情景下出现不可预测的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型广泛应用于学生评估，但很少有研究系统性地分析学生人口特征（如教育水平、社会经济情况）如何影响模型的评估和反馈，了解这一点对公平、公正评估尤为重要。

Method: 本研究设计了控制实验，包括显式（直接说明人口特征）和隐式（通过对话历史隐含人口信息）两种方式，在自动作文评分、形成性反馈和元语言问答三项任务中，使用六个先进的大型语言模型，考察人口统计特征对其输出的影响。

Result: 实验证明，无论是显性还是隐性人口提示，LLM都会识别并据此调整自己的输出。在显性场景下，模型会相应调整反馈的可读性；隐性场景下可能产生不可预测的偏见，例如降低对低教育水平学生的情感分数。

Conclusion: LLM在教育评估任务中对人口统计学特征具有敏感性，这既为个性化改进带来可能，也增加了歧视和偏见的风险。相关人员需谨慎考虑人口特征在自动化评估中的作用并防范潜在的不公。

Abstract: Large Language Models are now common in student assessment, but we know little about how student demographics affect their use. Sometimes, considering student demographics may be necessary -- for example, to improve readability for users with lower educational levels. However, it also risks being a cause of discrimination, e.g., when assigning lower scores to students from lower socioeconomic backgrounds. We set up controlled prompts to test 1) explicit demographic effects, where we mention demographic details directly, and 2) implicit effects, where we use conversation history as a demographic signal. We test these settings in three tasks: Automated Essay Scoring, Formative Feedback, and Metalinguistic Question Answering. We test six state-of-the-art LLMs on these tasks. In both explicit and implicit cases, the models pick up on demographic cues and can change their scoring, feedback, and answers accordingly. We find that LLMs frequently adjust the readability of feedback to education levels when these are explicitly mentioned. On the other hand, implicit conditions produce unpredictable biases, such as in question answering, where responses from lower-education levels receive lower sentiment scores. Our results provide clear evidence of demographic sensitivity in LLMs for educational assessment tasks.

</details>


### [150] [PaperDoctor: Evidence-Grounded and Actionable Feedback for Scientific Papers in Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16995)
*Kevin Qinghong Lin,Siyuan Hu,Pan Lu,Yu Chen,Yanzhe Chen,Owen Queen,Yupeng Chen,Jialin Yu,Junchi Yu,Zifeng Ding,Yuanfeng Ji,Sheng Liu,Jindong Gu,Linjie Li,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PaperDoctor是一个为学术论文提供递交前反馈的自动化智能代理系统，侧重于可审计和可操作的评论与建议，有效提升了论文自动化评审的质量和细致度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化学术研究代理在提升研究效率的同时，也容易让有缺陷的论文大规模进入学术文献。人工导师通常能通过详细且可追溯的反馈发现这些问题，但人工评审耗时且难以扩展。因此，亟需一种能够像人类导师一样、但更加自动化和可扩展的审核方法。

Method: 作者提出了PaperDoctor代理框架，分为三层：（1）L1层面为表面检查；（2）L2层由类型化验证器将每个论文主张与相关证据（如语句、公式、代码等）关联；（3）L3层根据主张重要性和计算预算，选择性重新构建并运行实验。每条反馈都包括观察发现、证据指向及修订建议。此外，系统可发现只读文章无法察觉的可重复性和量化局限。

Result: PaperDoctor在30篇写作中的论文上评估，得到了70.6%的反馈一致率和全部正向整体评分；在40篇跨领域、由人类及AI撰写并附代码的手稿上同样表现良好。整体来看，相比人工和其他代理型评审，PaperDoctor能生成更可审计、更具操作性的反馈，并补充了人工评审易忽略的维度。

Conclusion: PaperDoctor将自动化论文评审从“判决者”转变为“诊断者”，为实现更严格、更可追溯的AI辅助科学发现迈出了实质性一步。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents are reshaping the research ecosystem, but they can also let flawed claims enter the literature at scale. Human advisors catch such issues in drafts through careful, traceable feedback, yet advisor-style assessment requires extensive manual effort and does not scale. To shift automated paper assessment from a judge to a diagnostician, we introduce PaperDoctor, an agent framework for pre-submission feedback with three key innovations. First, a holistic hierarchical framework evaluates writing, layout, references, code, theory, prior work, and experiments through three layers: L1 surface screening, L2 typed verifiers that route each claim to the appropriate evidence, and L3 reproducers that rerun experiments by priority. Second, each finding contains an observation, a pointer to specific evidence such as a sentence, equation, or code line, and a revision suggestion, making critiques auditable and actionable. Third, PaperDoctor selectively rebuilds and reruns experiments based on claim importance and compute budget, surfacing reproducibility gaps and quantitative limitations that are invisible from the manuscript alone. We evaluate PaperDoctor on 30 in-progress papers, yielding 70.6% agreement and all positive holistic scores, and on 40 manuscripts across machine learning, natural science, and social science, covering human- and AI-authored papers with code. Overall, PaperDoctor produces more auditable feedback than human and other agentic reviewers, pairs critiques with concrete suggestions by design, and complements dimensions often overlooked by human reviewers. We also develop an interactive interface that lets authors browse findings grounded in their paper. PaperDoctor reframes automated paper assessment as diagnosis rather than verdict, taking a concrete step toward AI advisors for more rigorous AI-assisted scientific discovery.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs Follow the Pulse of a Crisis? Evaluating Crisis Sentiment in Bangladesh's July Uprising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16997)
*Md. Samiul Alim,Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Sharjil Khan,Rafia Ferdous Duti,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了UNRESTSENT200K，这是一个包含约20万条孟加拉危机期间社交媒体评论的大型情感数据集，并分析了不同语境和时序下的情感分析效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）在危机期间的情感分析尤为困难，因其语言、语境和公众反应变化迅速。目前缺乏高质量、规模化的数据集来支持相关研究。

Method: 收集了2024年孟加拉危机期间，来自Facebook和YouTube约20万条评论，涵盖五个事件阶段。所有评论由14名母语者人工标注，并进行高级验证，确保标注一致性。数据集中每条评论均关联至原帖文，可支持上下文分析。针对该数据集，基准测试了微调的编码器、大型语言模型（LLM）以及LoRA微调LLM。

Result: 实验证明，借助原帖文（父帖子）上下文，可显著提升情感分析模型性能；然而，不同危机阶段造成了显著的性能下降。强大的LLM模型总体表现良好，但在讽刺、隐含政治指涉和阶段依赖语义上仍有困难。

Conclusion: UNRESTSENT200K为低资源危机语境下的情感分析提供了宝贵的基准数据集，有助于开发更具上下文感知能力和时间鲁棒性的情感分析模型。

Abstract: Crisis sentiment analysis is especially challenging for low-resource languages such as Bangla, where language, context, and public reaction shift rapidly. We introduce UNRESTSENT200K, a Bangla crisis sentiment dataset with approximately 200K Facebook and YouTube comments from the July-August 2024 Bangladesh uprising. The dataset covers five event-aligned phases, from early escalation and internet blackout to regime transition and a later flood crisis. Each comment is linked to its parent post, enabling evaluation with and without discourse context. All comments are annotated through a fully human process involving 14 native Bangla-speaking annotators and senior validation, achieving substantial agreement (kappa = 0.73, alpha = 0.71) and 94.2% blind-audit agreement. We benchmark fine-tuned encoders, prompted LLMs, and LoRA-tuned LLMs. Results show that parent-post context consistently improves performance, while temporal shift across phases causes large performance drops. Strong LLMs perform well, but still struggle with sarcasm, implicit political references, and phase-dependent meaning. UNRESTSENT200K provides a benchmark for studying context-aware and temporally robust sentiment analysis in low-resource crisis discourse. UNRESTSENT200K is available at https://sami0055.github.io/UNRESTSENT200K/

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing the Fact-Grounding Gap in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17043)
*Kevin Mo,Nathan Mo,Richard Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多跳问答系统中错误类型，发现不仅仅是检索失败，还包括事实抽取失败，并提出这两类错误需分别应对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往多跳问答出错常归因于文档检索不全，但很少关注推理链条中各环节的具体失误原因。作者希望深入探查每一步推理时的错误类型，提升系统精度。

Method: 作者在三大多跳问答基准数据集上，分析每一步推理的错误，区分为检索失败（无需的段落未被检索到）和抽取失败（段落已检索到但信息抽取失败）。更进一步，验证各种检索改进方法后，考察抽取失败能否解决。

Result: 实验发现，抽取失败占据了近一半的推理步骤错误，仅靠检索方法改进无法解决抽取失败，且此现象在所有数据集和问题类型中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 多跳问答存在检索失败和抽取失败两类不同瓶颈，需采用不同评测和应对策略，而现有评测方法无法区分二者。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires combining information from multiple documents to answer complex questions. These systems have grown increasingly capable, yet when they fail, the error is typically attributed to not finding the right documents. Whether this holds at the level of individual reasoning steps remains largely unexamined. We investigate this across three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks and find that failures decompose into two distinct modes: retrieval failures, where the needed passage was not retrieved, and extraction failures, where the passage was retrieved but the needed fact could not be extracted - a phenomenon we term the fact-grounding gap. Extraction failures account for nearly half of all per-hop deficiencies and are invisible to standard retrieval metrics. They remain unresolved by every retrieval intervention we test, establishing a ceiling for retrieval-only improvements. The gap's severity varies across benchmarks and question types, but extraction failures appear on every dataset we measure. Our findings reveal that retrieval failures and extraction failures are fundamentally different bottlenecks requiring different solutions - a distinction absent from current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [153] [EviScope: Paired Counterfactual Evidence Diagnostics for Faithful and Efficient Grounded Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17081)
*Suryadeep Singh Deswal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EviScope基准，能够更细致地评估大语言模型对证据的实际依赖和推理能力，显示仅靠答案准确率无法反映模型在证据支持和冲突情境下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大语言模型多用最终答案的准确率衡量性能，但模型给出的正确答案可能并非真正由合适证据支持，甚至在证据不足或证据互相矛盾时也会给出答案。社区缺乏诊断模型证据依赖能力的细致工具。

Method: 作者提出EviScope基准，通过对同一问题的证据增删改（如添加干扰、移除支持、引入矛盾）构造四种对照情境，并为每种情境标注段落级支持信息。基于EviScope-v1.1，涵盖40组问题，共产生960条盲测生成数据，针对3种主流大模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 3.5 Flash）量化模型在不同证据配置下的推理表现。

Result: 实验揭示模型在证据依赖和冲突分辨上的巨大差异。例如，在本地开源模型上，显式的证据判门策略反而逊色于直接用RAG（Qwen由0.50降至0.15，Llama由0.375降至0.10）；Gemini整体表现优异，但在证据加入矛盾后依然有5%的情况给出答案。

Conclusion: EviScope能细致区分模型在缺乏支持、证据冲突、错误不回答等多种情景下的行为，比单纯评答案更能揭示模型推理和证据利用能力的本质差距。

Abstract: Grounded language-model systems are often evaluated by final answer accuracy, yet a correct answer can be unsupported, drawn from the wrong source, or produced when evidence is insufficient or contradictory. We introduce EviScope, a paired counterfactual benchmark that holds the question fixed while adding, removing, distracting, or contradicting its evidence. EviScope-v1.1 contains 40 four-condition quartets with repaired counterfactual claims and span-level support labels for automatic evaluation. Across 960 gold-blind generations from Qwen2.5-7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, paired metrics expose model-dependent grounding behavior that answer accuracy hides. On two local open models, an explicit evidence-action gate underperforms vanilla RAG on QCS: 0.15 vs. 0.50 for Qwen and 0.10 vs. 0.375 for Llama. Gemini reaches 0.944 joint success under both prompts, yet still answers 5% of conflict cases after contradiction insertion. EviScope therefore distinguishes unsupported answering, conflict blindness, and wrong non-answer actions rather than scoring answers alone.

</details>


### [154] [An Empirical Study of Counterfactual Self-Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17119)
*Giannis Kalyvas,Giorgos Filandrianos,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Vassilis Lyberatos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型生成自我解释时的可信度，特别是通过反事实自我解释来衡量其对实际决策行为的反映能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能够为自己的输出生成解释，但这些解释不一定忠实地反映模型实际的推理过程，因此需要量化验证其真实性。

Method: 采用反事实自我解释方法，对LLaMA-3和Qwen-2.5模型族中的十个指令微调模型，在情感分析和自然语言推理任务上，分别评估其解释的忠实度、最小性及与人工注释理由的一致性。

Result: 实验发现，模型规模越大，生成能改变自身预测并聚焦于决策相关证据的反事实解释的概率显著增大。理由引导条件可生成更加最小编辑、且更接近人类理由的反事实，但对忠实度提升并不稳定。

Conclusion: 反事实自我解释能为模型决策提供有价值的行为证据，但其可靠性高度依赖于模型容量，需要通过实证评估加以验证，而不能理所当然地假设其有效性。

Abstract: Large language models can easily generate explanations for their own outputs, but such self-explanations are not necessarily faithful to the model's behavior. We study this issue through counterfactual self-explanations, where a model minimally edits an input so that its own prediction changes. Across sentiment analysis and natural language inference, we evaluate ten instruction-tuned models from the LLaMA-3 and Qwen-2.5 families, measuring faithfulness, minimality, and alignment with human-annotated rationales. Our results show that model scale is the strongest determinant of explanation quality: larger models are substantially more likely to generate counterfactuals that flip their own predictions and target decision-relevant evidence. In contrast, the rationale-guided condition produces edit-minimal counterfactuals that are also more human-aligned. However, it does not consistently improve faithfulness. Overall, counterfactual self-explanations can provide useful behavioral evidence about model decisions, but their reliability depends strongly on model capacity and should be empirically validated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [155] [Psychological Effects of Cultural Upheavals from Millions of Song Lyrics Over 100 Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17225)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了百年间流行歌曲歌词，发现包括越战、9·11和新冠疫情等重大社会动荡后，艺术家歌词中自我指涉减少，认知加工词汇增加，创作主题也出现变化，体现了文化动荡对语言和社会的深远影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注文化动荡对语言的影响，但大规模分析其对流行媒体中个人行为的研究较少。本文希望填补这一空白。

Method: 作者评估了跨越百年的数百万首歌曲歌词，关注在越战、9·11恐袭和新冠疫情三次重大社会动荡前后，歌词中的自我指涉和认知加工词汇变化，并分析内容主题调整。

Result: 越战和9·11后，歌词中的自我指涉显著减少，认知加工词汇增加。歌词内容也由聚焦人际关系更多转向社会现象（如生命与自由）。

Conclusion: 文化动荡不仅改变了个人和集体的言语行为，也显示了其对社会的广泛影响。

Abstract: Cultural upheavals impact many aspects of social life, and many studies have investigated their impact on language patterns. However, few investigations have isolated the impact of upheavals on individuals at scale in popular media. The current work evaluated millions of song lyrics spanning more than a century in search of within-artist and between-artist signals of distress from the Vietnam War, the terrorist attacks of 9/11, and COVID-19. Compared to a five-year baseline, rates of self-references - a marker of psychological distancing - were significantly reduced after the Vietnam War and September 11th. Cognitive processing terms were elevated post-upheaval vs. pre-upheaval, which indicated artists' increased attempts to make meaning from such massive disruptions. Content patterns corroborated these findings as artists wrote more about "life and freedom" (societal conditions) and less about "courtship and nightlife" (interpersonal connection) following the upheavals. Cultural upheavals modify individual and collective verbal behavior, demonstrating their far-reaching impact on society.

</details>


### [156] [AraMIP: Extending MIPVU Towards Metaphor Identification in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17235)
*Mandar Marathe,Manar Ali,Sara Nabhani,Raia Abu Ahmad,Ibrahim Baroud,Omar Momen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了适用于阿拉伯语的隐喻识别规范（AraMIP），并基于改进的MIPVU流程对阿拉伯语比喻语言进行了系统注释和初步分析，使该领域资源更加规范化和标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语在比喻语言的识别和注释方面资源匮乏，缺乏统一的规范和大规模语料库，阻碍了相关研究的深入发展。

Method: 本研究基于MIPVU框架，结合阿拉伯语语言特点，开发了AraMIP注释指南。区分了三种阿拉伯语比喻类型（Isti'ara、kinaya、tashbih），并对300个句子进行注释分析。

Result: 发现了阿拉伯语比喻注释中的若干挑战，例如形态复杂性、词典义项排序不一致及缺少标准化语境材料。成功建立了首批具有代表性的注释数据集。

Conclusion: AraMIP作为阿拉伯语比喻识别的初步规范，有助于相关资源的标准化和可扩展性，为后续大规模比喻研究和数据集建设奠定了基础。

Abstract: Metaphor research has gained increasing attention due to its relevance to linguistic creativity, language use, cognitive processes, and related areas. While many efforts have been devoted to metaphor identification and annotation in English and other languages, Arabic remains under-resourced in this area. In this work, we propose the Arabic Metaphor Identification Procedure (AraMIP), a novel guideline for Arabic metaphor annotation. AraMIP builds on the widely used Metaphor Identification Procedure Vrije Universiteit (MIPVU) framework, incorporating adaptations that accounts for the language-specific properties of Arabic. We distinguish three major types of Arabic figurative language: Isti'ara (metaphor), kinaya (metonymy/indirect expression), and tashbih (simile), and annotate a pilot dataset of 300 sentences (5277 words). Our analysis reveals key challenges specific to Arabic, including morphological complexity, inconsistencies in dictionary sense ordering, and the absence of standardized contextual materials for annotators. This work contributes a first step toward standardized Arabic figurative instances and facilitates the development of larger annotated resources, thereby supporting future research on figurative language in Arabic.

</details>


### [157] [ECHO: Early-layer Collaborative Hierarchical Orchestration with Bonus Logits in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17241)
*Ziyang Ma,Zihong Zhang,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang,Baoyuan Qi,Siqi Li,Simin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO 提出一种层次化双循环推理框架，利用 LLM 层的功能差异，提高验证效率，加快推理速度，显著提升了大模型推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 draft-model-free speculative decoding 在 LLM 推理中受限于备用草稿候选的滞后性和高昂的验证计算成本，亟需高效验证与草稿生成新方法以提升 LLM 推理效率。

Method: ECHO 框架利用 LLM 层间功能差异，将推理过程分为高频的内部循环和低频的外部循环：内部循环利用早期层高判别效率快速、多步地探索草稿树，而外部循环通过状态复用实现最终层的权威验证，并用最终层 bonus logits 修正路径和补充高置信候选。

Result: 在多个 benchmark 测试中，ECHO 显著增加了平均被接受的 token 数，实现了 2.4×~2.9× 的加速效果，全面超越现有主流方法，几乎无需额外技术实现成本或部署参数。

Conclusion: ECHO 在无需增加额外参数和极少工程复杂度的前提下，大幅提升了 LLM 推理速度和效率，但在最优加速时需一次性微调。

Abstract: While draft-model-free speculative decoding offers a promising path to efficient LLM inference, it is frequently constrained by stale draft candidates and the high computational cost of the verification. To address these challenges, we propose ECHO, a hierarchical dual-loop framework that exploits the functional asymmetry between LLM layers. Leveraging the high discriminative efficiency of early layers and the authoritative distribution of final layers, ECHO bifurcates inference into a high-frequency inner loop and a low-frequency outer loop. Within the inner loop, early-layer bonus logits drive rapid, multi-step draft-tree exploration at a minimal cost. Simultaneously, the outer loop performs authoritative full-model verification through a state-reuse mechanism. Crucially, the outer loop also utilizes final-layer bonus logits to correct existing paths and supplement the tree with high-confidence candidates for subsequent cycles. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that ECHO significantly boosts mean accepted tokens and achieves a 2.4$\times$ to 2.9$\times$ speedup, outperforming existing state-of-the-art baselines with negligible engineering overhead and no extra deployment parameters, albeit with a one-shot fine-tuning dependency for optimal acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/whucs21Mzy/ECHO.

</details>


### [158] [Persistent Recurrent Memory Between Transformer Layers - Improves Language Model Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17251)
*Eduardo Novaes Hering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在decoder-only transformer中插入一个轻量级的持久循环状态（persistent recurrent state）模块，可以显著提升小型语言模型的泛化能力，且参数增加极少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统transformer（尤其解码器-only结构）在处理长距离依赖及泛化能力方面仍有局限，且容易过拟合。作者旨在通过引入循环记忆机制，提升模型对上下文信息的捕捉和泛化能力。

Method: 在一个6层transformer的下半部分和上半部分之间插入一个持久循环状态模块。该模块通过cross-attention接收隐藏表示，用GRU更新自身状态，再通过门控加法影响后续层。实验评估模块仅增加3.7%参数。

Result: 新结构在语言建模任务上将评估损失从2.438降至1.743（减少约28.5%），且此提升在5个随机种子下均统计显著（p<0.01），泛化差距也明显缩小。消融实验表明提升主要归因于记忆结构本身。

Conclusion: 在transformer中引入轻量级的循环持久记忆结构，可以高效提升小型语言模型的泛化能力，且几乎无需额外辅助损失。

Abstract: We introduce a simple architectural modification to decoder-only transformers: a persistent recurrent state that observes hidden representations via cross-attention, updates itself through a GRU, and modulates subsequent processing via gated addition. Inserted between the lower and upper halves of a 6-layer transformer, this module adds only 3.7\% additional parameters while reducing evaluation loss from $2.438 \pm 0.004$ to $1.743 \pm 0.018$, corresponding to a 28.5\% reduction on held-out language modeling data. The improvement is statistically significant across 5 random seeds ($p < 0.01$) and corresponds to reduced overfitting (generalization gap 0.12 vs 0.26). Through controlled ablations, we demonstrate that the improvement stems entirely from the persistent memory topology, not from auxiliary self-prediction objectives. A model with identical topology but no auxiliary loss performs equivalently, while a random auxiliary loss provides no benefit. Representation probing reveals that the persistent state encodes narrative position (52\% vs 33\% chance level)---information that standard attention maintains less efficiently. Our results suggest that bridging transformer layers with a lightweight recurrent memory is a simple, effective approach to improving generalization in small-scale language models.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Illusions Awareness in Cyber-Physical System's Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17260)
*Anna Di Placido,Nicolas Ferry,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出在CPS（信息物理系统）设计过程中，不仅要关注仿真与现实的差距（reality gap），更要系统性地识别和利用被现实“打脸”的设计假设，将其作为可复用的知识来改进系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS系统运行环境开放，难以预见所有情境，设计模型中隐含大量假设；这些假设一旦在运行时失效就形成现实与预期的差距（reality gap），导致模型不适用甚至风险。传统方法只试图缩小游戏与现实差距，并未系统性地对失效假设进行管理和复用。

Method: 提出“design illusions”概念，即对失效假设持续依赖下的错误信心，主张将这类失效假设显式化，并提出一套认知流程：识别、分类、表征这些design illusions，并将其转化为可行动的设计知识。

Result: 论文提出了一个“illusion-awareness”概念管道框架，帮助设计者系统地对失效假设进行管理和知识化利用，使得设计过程更具弹性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化、复用失效假设（design illusions），可以更好地支持CPS的设计决策，提升系统面对开放环境时的适应能力。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) operate through a continuous sense-compute-act loop within an open context environment, making it impossible to anticipate all the situations the system will face. To cope with this openness, stakeholders rely on assumptions, formalized into design models. However, these assumptions may no longer hold once the system is confronted with runtime reality, resulting in a discrepancy between expected and observed behaviour known in literature as the reality gap. Existing approaches mainly focus on reducing or overcoming it by making simulations more faithful to reality, with no unified methodology to structure and exploit invalidated assumptions that give rise to this gap as reusable design knowledge. We refer to the persistent reliance on invalidated assumptions -and the resulting false confidence in the design model's operational validity -as design illusions, and argue that they need to be made explicit, structured, and exploited as knowledge to support better design decisions. We propose a conceptual pipeline for illusions-awareness that identifies, classifies, characterizes, and leverages illusions to transform them into actionable design knowledge.

</details>


### [160] [Zero-shot narrative detection in social messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17310)
*Jesús M. Fraile-Hernández,Anselmo Peñas,Patrick Giedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究验证了大语言模型（LLM）无需训练样本即可有效识别社交消息中的隐含叙事，通过结合人工编写的描述和简单集成方法，性能可媲美有监督系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在社交媒体分析中，隐藏叙事检测依赖于大量有标注数据，而现实中大多数领域缺乏训练样本。研究动机是探索LLM是否能借助其丰富知识，零样本情况下辨认深层次叙事。

Method: 作者在Dipromats和SemEval数据集上进行实验，考察LLM在没有训练样本时，仅凭不同来源的叙事描述（人工和自动生成）或少量示例进行分类效果，并测试集成方法（如多数投票）。

Result: 实验结果表明，提供人工编写的叙事描述能显著提升分类性能，且优于自动描述与Few-shot示例。集成方法（尤其是多数投票）提高了鲁棒性。较大的模型表现最佳，且对提示语变化不敏感。

Conclusion: LLM具备零样本检测战略叙事能力，结合人工描述和简单集成后，效果可媲美有监督系统，为无训练数据的大量领域提供了可扩展的叙事检测解决方案。

Abstract: This study investigates the zero-shot ability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and classify hidden narratives in social messages. Our research hypothesis is that LLMs' extensive contextual knowledge allows them to interpret messages on a deeper, pragmatic level, going beyond basic sentiment or topic analysis. Experiments on the Dipromats and SemEval datasets show that providing models with human-written narrative descriptions significantly improves performance, without the need of training examples. In contrast, automatically generated descriptions or the use of few examples (few-shot) often degrade accuracy due to subtle shifts in framing. The study also finds that ensemble methods, particularly majority voting, enhance robustness and that larger models perform best while also being less sensitive to prompt variations. The findings validate that LLMs can effectively detect strategic narratives in a zero-shot setting, and when combined with simple ensembling and human-written descriptions, they can rival supervised systems, offering a scalable solution for narrative detection, specially when there is no training data for the vast majority of domains.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Detecting AI-Assisted Responses in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17317)
*Qizhou Wang,Bogdan Mamaev,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASURRE数据集，用于研究和检测大语言模型（LLM）辅助填写在线问卷的现象，涵盖从完全生成到基于角色模拟等多种策略，并评估了现有检测方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs被广泛用于协助在线问卷填写，这对基于问卷的研究有效性构成威胁。然而，如何检测LLM辅助填写问卷的行为仍缺乏研究。

Method: 作者构建ASURRE基准数据集，包含LLM以多种策略（如全自动回答、修改、角色扮演等）在三种不同学科的真实问卷上的作答；同时对比了人类作答。研究还系统评测了现有机器生成文本（MGT）检测器，并提出不同线索聚合的新检测方法。

Result: 现有检测器对于普通AI生成文本能有效识别，但对于角色扮演型LLM辅助时效果接近随机。尽管如此，AI无法完全模仿真实受访者的行为，依然留下独特行为痕迹，通过聚合多种线索的检测方法，AUROC提升了0.14。

Conclusion: LLM辅助填写问卷会对调查研究的有效性造成干扰，尤其是采用复杂模拟受访者的策略时检测更为困难，但通过多线索聚合的检测方式可显著提升识别能力。

Abstract: The use of LLMs to complete online surveys impacts the validity of survey-based research, but detecting such usage remains underexplored. We introduce an initial benchmark dataset, namely ASURRE, for AI-assisted survey participation to capture usage strategies ranging from full generation and revision to persona-grounded agentic completion. Controlled by these strategies, LLM-assisted survey responses are generated using multiple LLMs on three real-world surveys in different disciplines, paired with genuine human responses. Our evaluation of existing machine-generated text (MGT) detectors shows that naive AI usage is readily detectable, whereas persona-grounded agents that mimic entire respondents push detector performance toward chance. We further show that agentic completion cannot fully replicate respondent-level behaviour and leaves distinctive behavioural traces. While individual cues can be circumvented by targeted prompting, a simple few-shot, training-free aggregator over these cues improves mean AUROC by +0.14 over the best existing detector across agentic settings. Our project is available at https://github.com/mike-qz-wang/ASURRE.

</details>


### [162] [Vroom-Vroom at SHROOM-Visions: A Multi-Judge Committee for Detecting Hallucinated Spans in Vision-Language Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17327)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Nico Penttilä,Richard Schmidt,Arash Hajikhani,Victoria Palacin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多模型集成方法，用于检测和分类视觉-语言模型输出中的幻觉字符片段，并在SHROOM-Visions竞赛中取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在跨多语言场景下，输出中可能存在所谓的“幻觉”内容。为了提升幻觉内容检测与分类的准确性，研究者需要更有效的方法。该论文旨在提升幻觉内容识别的性能，并验证不同模型间的不一致性是否与人类注释者类似。

Method: 作者微调了多个视觉-语言模型，让它们作为独立的“评分员”对字符片段进行判断。然后利用字符级多数投票的方法合并各自的检测结果，并进一步探索了激活探针的应用。

Result: 所提出的方法在四种语言的任务中，有三种语言获得第一名，并且在所有语言和评价指标上都位居前三。

Conclusion: 分析结果表明，多个不同模型间的预测分歧与人类注释者之间的意见分歧有很强的相关性。该集成方法不仅有效提升了检测性能，还为理解模型间差异性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the SHROOM-Visions shared task on detecting and classifying hallucinated character spans in vision-language model outputs across four languages. We employ several fine-tuned vision-language models as independent annotators and combine their span predictions through character-level majority voting, and additionally explore activation probes. The approach ranks first in three of four languages and places on the podium in every language and metric. Our analysis indicates that disagreement among diverse models tracks disagreement among human annotators.

</details>


### [163] [Where Should a Document Live: Context, Representations, or Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17346)
*Nathanaël Carraz Rakotonirina,Momchil Hardalov,Gonzalo Iglesias,Adrià de Gispert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了LLM新知识注入的不同方法，发现基于KV-cache的Cartridges方法在大部分情形下精度最佳，但存在遗忘风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型需要获得训练外的新知识，目前存在多种注入方法（如上下文提供、参数微调、隐式注入），但各有利弊，缺乏系统对比。

Method: 论文在五个知识密集型基准上，系统对比了基于表示（KV-cache，Cartridges、Compaction）和基于参数（微调）的方法，包括理想和多文档检索两种场景。

Result: Cartridges（KV）在几乎所有存储预算下表现最好，比微调法高10分。Compaction与Cartridges仅在压缩率低时持平，超过50×时反而落后。现实场景中，只有Cartridges能达到ICL效果，并领先其他方法15-29分。然而，Cartridges与全量微调、大型MLP适配器一样，会产生灾难性遗忘，部分任务性能下降6-13%。

Conclusion: KV-cache的Cartridges方法在知识注入效率和效果上优于其他方案，但须权衡其遗忘风险。

Abstract: To answer questions outside of their pre-training data, large language models (LLMs) need access to new information, which can be presented in the context window as documents, encoded into the model's parameters, or injected as latent representations. However, each of these methods comes with different efficiency, cost, and performance trade-offs, with no single winner. We present a controlled comparison of representation-based (KV-cache based) and parametric (fine-tuning-based) adaptation methods on five knowledge-intensive benchmarks. We show that in the oracle setting, Cartridges (KV) are the most accurate injection method at nearly every storage budget, outperforming parametric methods by 10 points. Compaction (KV) matches Cartridges only at low compression rates, lagging behind the parametric methods by 10 points at rates higher than $50\times$. In the more realistic multi-document retrieval scenario, Cartridges are the only method that matches in-context learning (ICL), leading the parametric methods by 29 points and Compaction by 15 points. Nonetheless, Cartridges are also the only method, besides full fine-tuning and large MLP adapters, that suffers from catastrophic forgetting, i.e., a 6% performance degradation on control benchmarks, with 13% in coding.

</details>


### [164] [ECHO: A Matched-Contrast Benchmark for Context-Sensitive Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17360)
*Shuofeng Zhao,Hongwei Cai,Wenke Fan,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Zhou Wang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECHO——一个面向中文全双工对话系统的话语轮替诊断基准，能区分需让步与可继续说话的打断类型，并引入配对准确率评估新标准。实验显示，多数系统偏向让步，指出传统评测可能高估系统实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全双工对话系统需准确区分不同类型的重叠输入，如需要让步的打断与只需附和的backchannel。现有基准大多独立评估事件，不能有效考查模型的上下文敏感决策能力，容易让“总让步”或“总继续”这样的机械策略获得高分。

Method: 提出ECHO基准，将具有相同重叠语音文本但不同对话上下文的样例成对，每对中一条应选择让步，一条应选择继续，并加入部分无关对话样例，要求模型兼具区分能力。引入pair accuracy评估指标，只有配对两例都正确才算得分，对僵化决策零容忍。

Result: 在多个全双工系统评测中，绝大多数系统对让步（Yield）偏好明显，表现优于backchannel类情况，只有个别系统表现平衡。说明如果只做打断类评测，容易高估系统在复杂对话中的轮替能力。

Conclusion: ECHO基准更真实刻画全双工对话系统在自然对话中的性能，有助于更好地衡量和改进系统轮替决策能力，并将公开发布以促进研究。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems must distinguish interruptions that require yielding the floor from backchannels that permit continued speaking. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate events independently and may therefore reward fixed action preferences rather than context-sensitive decisions. We introduce ECHO, a paired diagnostic benchmark for Chinese full-duplex turn-taking. ECHO pairs examples with the same overlap transcript but contrasting preceding multi-turn dialogue contexts, with one requiring Yield and the other Keep. It additionally includes off-talk examples for diagnosing unnecessary yielding. We introduce pair accuracy, which requires correct decisions on both members of a pair and assigns no credit to constant-action policies. Experiments on multiple full-duplex systems show that most exhibit a pronounced bias toward \textsc{Yield}, performing substantially better on interruptions than on backchannels, while another system remains comparatively balanced. These findings demonstrate that interruption-only evaluation can overestimate practical turn-taking reliability. ECHO and its metadata will be publicly released.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancing Accessibility of Medical Texts through Large Language Model-Driven Plain Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17398)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出一种基于大型语言模型（LLMs）和Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）方法的自动医疗用语简化（PLA）方案，显著提高了医疗信息对患者的可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗文本晦涩难懂，患者阅读理解能力与医疗用语之间存在显著鸿沟。自动化简化技术能够使患者更好理解自身健康信息，提升医疗沟通效率。

Method: 采用先进的LLMs（GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro和LLaMA）进行文本简化，探索了零样本、少样本学习场景下的提示词策略，并通过QLoRA微调模型。此外，引入MoA方法提升系统适应性和健壮性。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM结合MoA技术的PLA方案能够有效简化医疗文本，在保持关键信息的前提下显著提升文本的可理解性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的PLA方法能够让复杂的医疗信息对患者更加友好，为提升医疗服务质量和患者体验提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of making complex healthcare information more accessible through automated Plain Language Adaptation (PLA). PLA aims to simplify technical medical language, bridging a critical gap between the complexity of healthcare texts and patients' reading comprehension. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), such as GPT and BART, have opened new possibilities for PLA, especially in zero-shot and few-shot learning contexts where task-specific data is limited. In this work, we leverage the capabilities of LLMs such as GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-1.5-pro, and LLaMA for text simplification. Additionally, we incorporate Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) techniques to enhance adaptability and robustness in PLA tasks. Key contributions include a comparative analysis of prompting strategies, finetuning with QLoRA on different LLMs, and the integration of MoA technique. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven PLA, showcasing its potential in making healthcare information more comprehensible while preserving essential content.

</details>


### [166] [Right Tool, Right Job: Native-Language Evaluation, Tokenizer Sensitivity, and Methodological Findings from a French-Only BabyLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17435)
*Adam Zachary Wasserman,David Beauchemin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种经过法语预训练的GPT-2模型MéTRON-FR，在法语语法和语言理解任务上表现良好，并通过多项跨语言和消融实验分析模型能力和诊断方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前小样本预训练模型多以英语为主，缺乏针对法语等其他语言的研究，且现有评测体系及分析方法不完全适用于法语等语言。作者希望通过在法语大语料上预训练GPT-2模型，探索其在法语语法、理解能力及跨语言任务中的表现，同时提升小模型在非母语环境下的诊断与评估方法。

Method: 作者预训练了一个125M参数的GPT-2模型（MéTRON-FR），在9247万法语词语料上训练，并在QFrBLiMP、BabyLM-weighted leaderboard等基准测试。采用跨语言GLUE协议，将任务数据翻译为法语并结合rank-16 LoRA微调，并通过Bilingual Lexicon Induction方法评估嵌入对齐情况。此外进行了消融实验，分析tokenizer和prompt模板对模型零样本预测的影响。

Result: MéTRON-FR在QFrBLiMP（法语语法对比任务）上取得85.97%的高分，BabyLM权重榜获得62.80%。跨语言GLUE实验显示，关系类任务表现有提升，但涉及世界知识的任务表现下降。词典诱导实验中，法语与英文GPT-2嵌入对齐精度显著高于随机水平。消融研究发现，在小模型下，tokenizer和模板对结果影响显著。

Conclusion: 作者证明了在中小规模下训练的法语GPT-2可以获得良好的语法能力，并提出tokenizer敏感性、安慰剂控制的prompt和母语配对基准应成为低资源小模型的标准诊断方法，有助于更准确地评估和提升模型性能。

Abstract: We submit MéTRON-FR, a 125M GPT-2 pretrained on 92.47M words of French, to the BabyLM 2026 Strict track. It scores 85.97 +/- 0.17% on QFrBLiMP (a native Quebec-French benchmark of grammatical minimal pairs) and 62.80% on the BabyLM-weighted leaderboard. A cross-lingual GLUE (General Language Understanding Evaluation) protocol that combines French task-data translation with rank-16 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) produces a sharp task-type gradient: relational tasks gain measurably, while world-knowledge tasks regress. Bilingual Lexicon Induction aligns the French embeddings to GPT-2 at p@1 = 68.84 +/- 8.61%, 18X above chance, suggesting cross-lingual alignment tracks acquired grammatical competence rather than training duration. An ablation study shows that single-token zero-shot scoring is dominated by tokenizer and template artifacts at the child scale, motivating tokenizer-swap sensitivity, placebo-controlled prompting, and native-language minimal-pair benchmarks as standard diagnostics.

</details>


### [167] [What Breaks Under Pruning in Smart Homes, and When? Evaluating LLM Degradation Across Architectures and Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17515)
*Congjing Zhang,Vashishtha Patil,Henning Lange,Usman Aleem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究了剪枝对大语言模型（LLMs）在智能家居工具调用任务中的影响，发现不同模型和剪枝方法表现差异显著，MoE模型对剪枝更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然剪枝有助于降低大语言模型的部署成本，但其对实际基于上下文的工具调用任务，特别是在智能家居场景下的具体影响尚不清楚。因此，作者希望系统分析各种模型结构和剪枝方式下任务表现的变化规律。

Method: 作者在四种大语言模型（涵盖Dense Transformer、Dense Hybrid、MoE等架构）和四种剪枝方式（深度、宽度、混合、专家剪枝）上进行对比实验。剪枝后，模型通过有监督微调（SFT），随后在三个智能家居数据集的19,500多个实例上评测。分析不仅考虑整体准确率，还细致考察了动作组成部分和任务复杂性的影响。

Result: Dense结构模型存在较窄的“安全剪枝区间”，超出后准确率骤降，而MoE模型能承受更大比例的剪枝。剪枝首先损害具体参数生成的准确性，其次才影响意图理解；对Dense模型过度剪枝还会使模型经常拒绝执行任务。

Conclusion: 剪枝不仅影响整体准确率，更会导致工具调用任务中更细粒度表现的退化。因此，在挑选剪枝后的LLMs用于实际任务时，必须超越单一准确率，系统评估其可靠性。

Abstract: Pruning can reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), but its impact on context-grounded tool calling remains poorly understood. We systematically study pruning-induced degradation in smart-home tool calling across four LLMs spanning dense Transformer, dense hybrid, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, together with depth, width, hybrid, and expert pruning methods. After post-pruning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we evaluate more than 19,500 instances from three smart-home datasets. Beyond aggregate task accuracy, we characterize degradation along two dimensions: action components (i.e., operation, device, argument, and value) and task complexity. Our results show that dense models have narrow safe pruning regions followed by sharp degradation, while MoE models tolerate substantially more pruning. Pruning degrades grounded specificity before schema-level intent, and aggressive dense pruning can induce systematic over-refusal. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating pruning beyond aggregate accuracy when selecting pruned LLMs for reliable tool execution.

</details>


### [168] [When Should LLMs Abstain? Chain-of-Self-Questioning for Selective Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17516)
*Ali Şenol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了链式自我提问（CoSQ）方法，通过明确评估信息支持来决定大模型是否做出答案承诺，在多个模型和条件下显著降低了错误承诺率，并提升了答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在事实支持薄弱时依然能产出流畅但错误的答案，因而需要有机制减少大模型做出没有足够支持的信息承诺。

Method: 作者提出了三种基于提示的链式自我提问（CoSQ）方法，使模型在回答问题前先自我检视现有信息能否支撑答案，再决定是否回答，并在 TruthfulQA 数据集上的多个模型和设定下进行评测。

Result: Grounded-CoSQ 在最终协议下，将错误承诺率从13.1%降至8.9%，提升答案准确率到89.7%，并保持87.6%的问题都能给出答案。其他 CoSQ 变体也取得提升，并在所有十一种大模型和不同阈值下都有效。

Conclusion: 自我评估（CoSQ）方法能有效实现大模型根据信息基础决定是否回答，显著降低不可靠答案的发生，对于需要高可靠性的场景极具应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent answers when their factual support is weak. This paper introduces Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ), a prompt-only framework that makes answer commitment conditional on an explicit assessment of the information required to answer a question. We evaluate three CoSQ variants under seventeen conditions on the 817-item TruthfulQA multiple-choice validation set using eleven open-weight and hosted model families. In the final balanced-option protocol, Grounded-CoSQ at τ=0.90 reduces the mean unconditional wrong-commitment rate from 13.1% under chain-of-thought prompting to 8.9%, a 32.1% relative reduction, while increasing answered accuracy from 86.9% to 89.7% and answering 87.6% of questions. Both improvements hold for all eleven models and at every evaluated threshold. Critical-CoSQ and Adaptive-CoSQ provide neighboring operating points with 88.6% and 86.5% coverage, respectively, while remaining more reliable than the baseline. A secondary Natural Questions Short-Answer evaluation provides convergent open-form evidence. These findings show that self-assessment can support explicit, tunable answer-or-abstain decisions when an unsupported commitment is more costly than referral or review.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Bi-MoDe: Bilateral Control-based Imitation Learning via Modifier-Conditioned Decoding for Modulation of Execution Speed and Contact Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16040)
*Takumi Kobayashi,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Bi-MoDe的模仿学习方法，通过向Transformer模型注入受限潜变量，使操作者能在推理阶段灵活控制机器人的任务执行方式(如速度和力度)，在白板擦拭任务中提升了物理指令的遵循性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的双边控制模仿学习方法虽然能够捕捉位置和力信息，适合处理接触丰富的操作任务，但很难让操作者在执行时灵活指定任务细节（如快慢、轻重等）。这是实际机器人操作中重要但未被很好解决的需求。

Method: 提出了Bi-MoDe框架，将被约束的潜在变量作为修饰符通过adaLN-Zero机制注入到Transformer动作解码器的每一层，实现指令对动作分块生成的直接影响。通过物理和时序修饰符对擦白板任务进行实验，并设计消融实验分析各模块作用。

Result: Bi-MoDe在物理指令执行方面相较于仅动作分块的基线方法有明显提升，同时保持了类似的时序控制能力。消融实验显示，解码器调制与潜空间组合的结合对于实现精确物理指令遵循至关重要。

Conclusion: Bi-MoDe框架能够实现对机器人操作过程中物理和时序行为的灵活控制，提升了现实接触丰富操作任务中对物理指令的响应能力，为通用性更强的模仿学习方法提供了新方案。

Abstract: Bilateral control-based imitation learning captures both position and force information, making it well suited to contact-rich manipulation. However, existing approaches provide limited means for an operator to specify how a learned task should be executed at inference time, such as slowly or quickly, gently or firmly. We propose Bi-MoDe, a modifier-conditioned decoding framework that injects a constrained latent into every layer of the Transformer action decoder via adaLN-Zero, allowing behavioral directives to directly influence action-chunk generation. We evaluate the method on a real-world whiteboard wiping task with combinations of temporal and physical modifiers. Bi-MoDe improves physical directive following over the action-chunking baseline while maintaining comparable temporal control. An ablation further shows that decoder conditioning and latent-space composition interact, and that their combination is important for accurate physical directive following. Additional material is available at the https://mertcookimg.github.io/bi-mode/

</details>


### [170] [MR-GLi: Mixed Reality-Based Gripper-Linked Overlays for Underwater Robot Arm Teleoperation via Bilateral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16041)
*Masashi Sasago,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于水下遥操作的MR界面MR-GLi，并与传统2D显示器效果对比。结果显示MR界面在反馈获取和主观负担等方面有独特优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉力反馈对水下双向遥操作很重要，但混合现实（MR）在信息呈现上的优势尚不明确。因此，研究动机是评估MR相较于常规2D显示的实际效益。

Method: 开发了MR-GLi，将反作用力矩指示器和手腕摄像头画面空间注册到机器人夹持器。招募20名被试，在相同视觉信息和四通道双向控制条件下，完成搬运刚性/柔性物体的对比实验。

Result: MR-GLi在扭矩调节表现上与2D监视器相当，但能让操作员更便捷地获取反馈，同时降低了在工作区和监控画面之间切换注意力的主观负担。

Conclusion: 通过MR-GLi验证了夹持器绑定MR覆盖在水下遥操作中的可行性，突显了信息获取方式对操作体验的重要性。

Abstract: Visual torque feedback supports underwater bilateral teleoperation, but the benefit of mixed reality (MR) over conventional monitor presentation remains unclear. We present MR-GLi, an MR interface that spatially registers a reaction torque indicator and wrist-camera image to the robot gripper. Twenty participants performed lift and pick-and-place tasks with rigid and compliant objects in a counterbalanced within-subject comparison with a 2D monitor, using identical visual-feedback content and four-channel bilateral control. MR-GLi provided gripper-linked access to visual feedback while maintaining a similar level of torque-regulation performance to the 2D monitor. Subjective evaluation further indicated reduced perceived burden associated with shifting attention between the workspace and visual feedback. These results demonstrate the feasibility of gripper-linked MR overlays for underwater bilateral teleoperation and highlight the importance of considering information access in addition to task performance. Additional material: https://mertcookimg.github.io/mr-gli/

</details>


### [171] [World-Action Models for Robot Learning and Control: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16074)
*Zuxing Lu,Hongjia Zhai,Guanzhi Wang,Huajian Zeng,Jiaqi Yang,Jingyu Liu,Lei Cheng,Yuantai Zhang,Yuheng Qiu,Zezhou Cheng,Ivan Laptev,Danfei Xu,Benjamin Riviere,Giuseppe Loianno,Eric Xing,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器人领域中的世界-动作模型（WAMs），系统梳理了相关方法、应用场景与未来挑战，旨在为实现更可靠的机器人智能提供技术参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在复杂环境下仅依靠观测和指令映射难以实现高效决策，需要兼具对未来世界的预测与动作生成，以提升任务完成的可靠性和适应性。

Method: 本文通过围绕WAMs的相关技术进行系统性梳理，将现有方法根据表示、状态转移建模、动作接口、体系结构、训练流程、数据类型及扩展性策略分类，并详细评述其在操作、导航和自动驾驶等场景的应用。同时，总结了常用的数据集、评测指标和协议，对比了WAMs与传统世界模型、基于模型的强化学习、动态图像生成及反应式视觉-语言-动作策略的区别。

Result: 文章建立了WAMs的统一技术框架和分类法，对已有研究进行了详尽归纳，对比了各种实现路线与性能，梳理了应用实例和实际评测情况，全面呈现了该领域的现状。

Conclusion: WAMs作为集成世界模型和动作生成的前沿技术，对于实现更具可靠性的机器人智能至关重要。当前尚存在动作与世界对齐、因果分解、空间一致性、长时记忆及高效推理等挑战，未来需持续攻关。本文为领域研究者系统性认识和深入研究WAMs提供了基础参考。

Abstract: Robots operating in open environments act under partial observability, physical constraints, and dynamic task contexts. Beyond mapping observations and language instructions to actions, they must anticipate how candidate actions may affect future states and task-relevant outcomes. Recent advances in world models, video generation, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have motivated the development of World-Action Models (WAMs), which couple future world prediction with executable action generation. This survey provides a robotics-oriented review of WAMs. We clarify their scope relative to conventional world models, model-based reinforcement learning, action-conditioned video generation, and reactive VLA policies, and organize existing methods through a unified taxonomy covering representations, transition modeling, action interfaces, architectures, training pipelines, data modalities, and scaling strategies. We further review applications of WAMs in manipulation, navigation, and autonomous driving, and we summarize the datasets, benchmarks, metrics, and protocols used to evaluate WAM systems. Finally, we discuss key challenges in action alignment, world-action factorization, spatial and multi-view consistency, long-horizon memory, neural simulation for closed-loop policy learning, and efficient inference. Taken together, this survey aims to provide a concise technical foundation for integrating predictive world modeling with action generation, toward more reliable embodied robot intelligence. Project page: https://rcl-robotics.github.io/Awesome-World-Action-Models.

</details>


### [172] [AssemblyGrid v1: A Benchmark for Multi-Robot Production with Temporary Coalitions, Local Information, and Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16075)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,David Heik,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于多机器人柔性生产的基准平台AssemblyGrid v1，支持研究机器人在部分可观测和资源竞争下的合作决策，并评估了多种决策方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在柔性机器人生产中，生产流程、物料流转、资源分配、合作任务等决策高度耦合，且在去中心化的控制下更为复杂。缺乏统一且可复现的基准平台限制了相关方法的研究与公平对比。

Method: 建立了AssemblyGrid v1基准，设计了流程进展、去中心化可观测、物料转移、多机器人临时组队、并发执行和几何可行性等任务特性，划分了三种工作负载（Flow、Coalition、Concurrency），提供多个场景难度，评测指标独立于具体解法。采用中心化基准、结构化去中心化控制器和多智能体强化学习（如IPPO、MAPPO、QMIX）进行实验。

Result: 实验证明，在中心化与去中心化控制下均可实现高效生产行为。多智能体强化学习能仅凭局部观测与动作学会有效生产策略，验证了各类算法的适用性。

Conclusion: AssemblyGrid v1为研究多机器人在去中心化和部分可观测等复杂条件下的合作决策提供了统一、可复现的基准环境，有助于方法开发和深入机制探索。

Abstract: Flexible robotic production requires joint decisions on process progression, material routing, resource assignment, temporary cooperation, and simultaneous execution, since each decision can affect the feasibility of the others. The challenge is greater under decentralized control, where each robot acts from bounded local information while system progress depends on collective decisions, shared resources, material state, and workspace compatibility. These properties closely match cooperative multi-agent decision making under partial observability and resource contention. This paper introduces AssemblyGrid v1, a reproducible benchmark for repeated multi-robot production that combines explicit process progression, decentralized observations, material transfer, temporary multi-robot coalitions, productive concurrency, and geometry-dependent feasibility within one task-level formulation. The benchmark includes Flow, Coalition, and Concurrency workload families, each with three scenario levels. Task success and evaluation measures are defined independently of learning reward and solution method, allowing learning-based and non-learning methods to address the same production problem. AssemblyGrid v1 is evaluated through executable conformance checks, mechanism studies, and algorithmic experiments using a privileged centralized reference, structured decentralized controllers, and MARL methods including IPPO, MAPPO, and QMIX. Results demonstrate productive execution under centralized and decentralized control. The MARL experiments further show that decentralized policies can learn effective production behavior from local observations and actions, supporting AssemblyGrid as a controlled benchmark for studying cooperative decision making in flexible robotic production.

</details>


### [173] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Circuit Architectures for Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16089)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子电路架构，用于表示和实现刚体变换，特别适用于Denavit–Hartenberg（DH）动力学建模的串联开链机器人系统。该方法能够在有限深度的量子硬件上实现，并能保持几何关系和可观测量的物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间几何数据在量子计算中的编码难以兼顾几何关系的保留、可观测量的暴露和在实际量子硬件上的可实现性。机器人动力学的刚体变换表示和求解对高效的计算资源有着迫切需求。因此，研究能在有限量子资源下实现高效、精确的几何编码方法具有重要现实意义。

Method: 方法将每一个平移分量分解为经典的度量模和一个以单量子比特Bloch向量编码的有符号方向；旋转部分则通过参数化门模拟DH变换中坐标轴的有序传播。通过额外的寄存器，实现各分量概率幅度的归一化加权和编码。通过不同的量子电路结构，对比了量子比特数量、线路深度和控制操作等多方面的实现折中。

Result: 理想仿真条件下，电路重建末端执行器的位置和姿态误差极小。进行了有限次数测量、有噪声模拟和真实量子硬件实验，评估了统计误差、噪声敏感性及实际实现的资源开销。各种电路结构在不同约束下实现了相同的几何表示。

Conclusion: 新提出的量子表示和电路架构能够高效、精确地在量子系统中实现机器人串联臂DH变换的空间关系，在现有量子硬件上保持物理几何意义和可观测性，为未来量子辅助机器人动力学及空间几何计算提供了重要方法基础。

Abstract: Structured spatial data require quantum encodings that preserve geometric relations, expose measurable observables, and remain implementable on finite-depth hardware. This work introduces a quantum representation and circuit architecture for rigid-body transformations and specializes it to Denavit--Hartenberg kinematics of serial open-chain manipulators. Each translational contribution is factorized into a classical metric magnitude and a signed unit direction encoded by a single-qubit Bloch vector, while parameterized rotations reproduce the ordered propagation of frame directions. A selector register prepares probabilities proportional to the contribution magnitudes, and the reduced state of a designated readout qubit encodes their normalized weighted sum. The retained classical scale then reconstructs the metric end-effector position. Two additional readout qubits encode terminal-frame axes, providing a compact and geometrically interpretable pose interface. At the ideal expectation-value level, measured Pauli observables reproduce the corresponding classical kinematic quantities. Alternative circuit architectures realize the same representation with different tradeoffs in qubit count, circuit depth, controlled operations, and measurement requirements. Validation on a serial manipulator yields numerically negligible position and orientation reconstruction errors under ideal simulation. Finite-shot simulations, noisy executions, transpilation analysis, and a hardware demonstration further characterize statistical error, noise sensitivity, and implementation overhead without asserting computational advantage.

</details>


### [174] [Occupancy Network-Guided Autonomous Robotic Partial Nephrectomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16186)
*Ethan Kilmer,Pit Henrich,Jiawei Ge,Paul M. Scheikl,Laura Connolly,Soum D. Lokeshwar,Joseph Chen,Justin D. Opfermann,Kaitlyn Kumar,Lauren Shepard,Ahmed Ghazi,Nirmish Singla,Richard J. Cha,Kevin Cleary,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了首个能够自主进行肿瘤完整切除（部分肾切除）的视觉引导自主外科系统，首次实现对不断变形和切割的软组织的识别和主动适应，并在体外实验获得了很好的切除效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主软组织外科手术系统只限于器官表面的简单干预，无法应对手术中器官变形或切割后组织结构的变化。因此，亟需一种能够感知3D解剖结构动态变化、并对其自适应的自主手术解决方案，以提升真实临床应用的可行性。

Method: 研究团队提出了一种视觉引导的自主外科系统：1）构建并在物理仿真中训练条件占用网络，该网络能够根据深度相机采集的单视角点云，推断出完整的三维解剖结构，并在切割和变形过程中保持实时追踪；2）双机械臂分别承担电切割与吸持作用；3）自主控制策略配合上述感知系统，实现肿瘤切除规划和执行。

Result: 在模拟手术场景（病人衍生水凝胶模型）下，机器人完成了8次自主肿瘤切除（共77次电切割），全部切口均取得了阴性手术边界，平均绝对边界误差为1.61±0.48毫米。

Conclusion: 该系统首次验证了在体外模型上，实现了基于成像闭环驱动、自主监督的阴性边界肿瘤切除，为未来临床自主手术奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Autonomous soft-tissue cancer surgery has been limited to interventions on organ surfaces, because current systems cannot perceive and adapt to anatomy once it deforms or is cut. We introduce the first vision-guided autonomous system capable of performing complete tumor resections for partial nephrectomy. Our system integrates conditional occupancy networks, trained entirely in a physics-based simulation, that infer full 3-D anatomy (tumor, margin tissue, and kidney) from single-view partial point clouds. These occupancy networks maintain intraoperative tracking even as tissue is cut and deformed, enabling adaptive planning and execution. The surgical platform combines a depth camera for capturing surface point clouds, dual robotic arms for electrosurgical cutting and vacuum-based tissue manipulation, and an autonomous control strategy for tumor resection. In patient-derived hydrogel phantoms under an open partial nephrectomy setting, the robot performed eight consecutive autonomous tumor resections comprising 77 electrosurgical cuts, with all cuts achieving negative surgical margins and 1.61 $\pm$ 0.48 mm mean absolute margin error. This work demonstrates, for the first time, a foundation for supervised autonomous closed-loop, imaging-driven, margin-negative tumor removal in phantoms.

</details>


### [175] [Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot with Modular Stiffness and In-Situ Self Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16256)
*Guo Ning,Sue,Zheng Cao,Junzhe Hu,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Tiancheng Wu,Zackory Erickson,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可自我感知、模块化且机械结构可重构的连续体机器人平台，可在无需外部传感设备的情况下实现形态变化和定位，并已通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的连续体机器人多为任务特定，且依赖外部传感设施，导致实际部署和适应性受限。因此，亟需一种可独立工作的模块化连续体机器人，提升灵活性与实用性。

Method: 该机器人采用可更换的连续体关节，每个关节的刚度通过分析预先计算好，实现快速组装与形态编程。姿态估计依赖磁传感器和模块化学习框架，每个关节训练一个模型并在不同配置中复用。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在真实环境中具备自我感知能力和形态适应性，无需依赖外部追踪系统即可运行。

Conclusion: 本文提出的机器人平台突破了对外部传感器的依赖，并借助模块化与学习算法实现了高适应性和可重构性，具有广泛的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Continuum robots enable smooth shape morphing and safe interaction in confined environments. However, most existing systems are task-specific and depend on external sensing infrastructure, limiting their adaptability and real-world deployment. This paper presents a self-contained modular continuum robotic platform that combines mechanical reconfigurability with onboard pose estimation. The robot is constructed from interchangeable continuum joints with analytically precomputed stiffness, allowing rapid assembly and direct programming of the robot shape. Proprioceptive sensing is achieved using magnetic sensors and a modular learning-based framework, where a single model is trained per joint and reused across configurations. The system is experimentally validated in real world, demonstrating self-sensing capabilities and adaptation without external tracking.

</details>


### [176] [ConGraspXL: Controllable Constraint-Conditioned Dexterous Grasping Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16319)
*Hui Zhang,Mirko Meboldt,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConGraspXL，一种可控的抓取动作合成方法，支持多样的任务约束并精确、灵活地控制，实现了对各种单一或组合约束的抓取动作合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取合成方法（如GraspXL）缺乏对特定任务驱动多样约束（如接触区域、手腕轨迹等）的可控性，限制了其在实际应用的适用性。

Method: 提出了ConGraspXL，将约束条件以层次化方式建模，并引入了带掩码的残差接口（masked residual interface）用于灵活组合约束，同时通过动态手心定位和前馈手腕引导提升控制精度。

Result: 在不损失GraspXL强泛化能力的前提下，ConGraspXL实现了对多种单一或组合约束下的抓取动作的精细、灵活控制。

Conclusion: ConGraspXL为多约束和多任务抓取提供了可控的、低层级的动作控制器，有望支持全身抓取、功能性抓取及人类动作模仿等多种应用场景。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping is usually conducted for specific tasks, leading to heterogeneous constraints such as specific approach directions, desired contact regions, specified wrist trajectories, and functional hand poses. Our previous work, GraspXL, achieves scalable grasping motion synthesis for diverse objects and hand morphologies, while lacking controllability for synthesis under such various task-driven constraints. In this paper, we propose ConGraspXL, which extends GraspXL with controllable constraint-conditioned grasp motion synthesis that accommodates diverse task-driven constraints and their combinations. We introduce a hierarchical constraint formulation, enable flexible constraint composition with a masked residual interface, and improve control precision with dynamic hand centers and feed-forward wrist guidance. Without losing the strong generalization capabilities of GraspXL, ConGraspXL enables precise and flexible controllability for various individual constraints and their combinations, providing a plug-and-play low-level grasp controller for downstream applications such as whole-body grasp completion, functional grasping, and human-motion imitation.

</details>


### [177] [ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16331)
*Peiqi Yu,Mosam Dabhi,Shangtao Li,Bowei Li,Laszlo Jeni,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需示范且具有模块化能力的机器人操作框架 ManiSkillFormer，通过引入任务条件的几何合约，将感知和动作明确分离，提升了多任务与多物体的操作泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法依赖端到端的视觉运动策略及大量示范数据，难以泛化到新任务或新物体，且缺乏模块化、可组合性。本工作旨在实现无需演示、支持多任务和多物体的机器人操作新范式。

Method: 引入任务条件几何合约，将人类定义的技能结构与大语言模型 (LLM) 结合，让 LLM 生成针对不同任务与对象的合约和动作模板。感知模块根据合约从观测中提取三维语义几何特征（如关键点、法线），并用这些参数实例化存储技能库中的动作模板，无需端到端训练。

Result: 在Galaxea R1-Lite双臂机器人上，方法在三种设置下均优于对照组和删减版流程：无演示抓放任务平均成功率88.24%；功能性操作任务（如旋开、倒液、按压、折叠）成功率75%；三项长序列任务完成率达50-80%。

Conclusion: ManiSkillFormer 能够实现在无需人工演示及逐对象策略微调的前提下，模块化、复用且泛化的机器人操作技能，为通用机器人操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present ManiSkillFormer, a neuro-symbolic framework for demonstration-free and compositional robotic manipulation. Instead of learning end-to-end visuomotor policies, ManiSkillFormer introduces task-conditioned geometric contracts that explicitly structure the interface between perception and action. Each manipulation skill declares the semantic geometric primitives required for execution, such as object keypoints and surface normals. Building on human-defined skill structures, LLM agents generate these contracts and corresponding motion templates for different objects and task contexts. These contracts guide the perception module to ground task-relevant 3D primitives from observations, which are then used to instantiate reusable motion templates stored in a skill library. We evaluate ManiSkillFormer on Galaxea R1-Lite dual-arm robot across three settings: zero-shot pick-and-place over 8 object categories with 30 different instances, functional manipulation tasks including unscrewing, pouring, pressing, and folding, and 3 long-horizon tasks. ManiSkillFormer achieves higher average success rates than the evaluated baselines and two ablated pipelines: 88.24% for demonstration-free pick-and-place, 75.00% average success on functional manipulation and 50--80% completion rates across the long-horizon tasks. These results show that our design enables composable and reusable manipulation across objects and tasks without per-object policy fine-tuning or additional robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [178] [Auto-HSI: Personalized human control of a robot swarm on demand by using LLMs for online automatic code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16346)
*Alessandro Nazzari,Nathan Cerisara,Dorian Tonnis,Raina Zakir,Lorenzo Labarile,Weixu Zhu,Marco Dorigo,Mary Katherine Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Auto-HSI方法，能够根据自然语言和手势演示，为用户按需生成个性化的人-群体机器人交互界面，实现无需编程即可通过手势操控多个机器人集体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-群体机器人交互界面常常需要专业知识和复杂的编程，不适合非专业用户；因此，论文旨在简化交互流程，让非专家通过自然语言和手势即时定制多机器人集体行为控制方案。

Method: 用户通过自然语言描述和手势演示表达需求，系统基于这些输入自动生成个性化状态机代码，并为集中式控制界面生成交互逻辑，支持单手和双手操作。论文用原型系统Auto-HSI实现，并对手势追踪、代码生成性能及整体系统进行基准和仿真实验。

Result: Auto-HSI在实验中实现了50台机器人在实际物理仿真环境中集中控制，包括进球、通过变形穿越迷宫，以及机器人分组同时完成两个目标。系统成功支持实时手势追踪、动态界面更新，并在仿真和实体机器人上均有成功演示。

Conclusion: Auto-HSI实现了自然语言和手势驱动的群体机器人个性化交互界面自动生成，降低了非专业用户参与群体机器人控制的门槛，具有良好的扩展性和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents Auto-HSI, a method for generating personalized human-swarm interaction (HSI) interfaces on demand. The objective is to enable untrained operators to use natural language descriptions and gesture demonstrations to explain how they want the robots to collectively behave in response to their gestures. Based on these inputs, the code should automatically be generated for personalized state machines that will control the robots as desired, in response to the desired gesture inputs. In the developed Auto-HSI prototype, the generated code produces a personalized interface for centralized control using one- and two-handed gestures, enabling a user to teleoperate the robots' motion, formation shape, and shape deformation. We test the gesture tracking and code generation components of Auto-HSI against performance benchmarks. We then test the full Auto-HSI prototype in ``live'' operation experiments, in which real human operators centrally control 50 simulated robots in a physics-based simulator, under nominal and noisy conditions. In these experiments, robots are teleoperated to: score a goal, traverse a maze that requires shape deformation, and score two simultaneous goals by splitting into two groups. We also demonstrate a real human operator making live updates to their personalized Auto-HSI interface during operation (in simulation). Finally, we demonstrate live operation of real robots.

</details>


### [179] [UDAV: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16368)
*Ghazal Farhani,Shabnam Shabani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了UDAV，一种结合不确定性驱动与视觉-语言模型（VLM）的无人机(UAV)辅助无人车(UGV)路径规划方法，有效提升了路径预测的准确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的越野路径规划缺乏对预测结果可靠性的度量，易导致生成路径存在大误差，限制了实际部署。

Method: UDAV通过多次随机生成路径预测，选取最具代表性的中位本道路作为最终路线，同时通过这些预测的空间分布估算不确定性。若路线的不确定性超过设定阈值，则激活重新评估环节，否则直接返回中位本道路。

Result: 在400条实际航拍路径测试中，UDAV平均位移误差（ADE）由传统方法的147.4像素降至110.4像素，误差降低25.1%。同时在90%和95%分位点误差方面也优于其他方法。

Conclusion: UDAV不仅提升了VLM规划路径的准确性，还为规划过程提供了可靠的不确定性信号，可有效规避大规划误差，增强系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate routes directly from aerial imagery for off-road navigation, but their predictions provide no indication of reliability. We present UDAV, an Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner for UAV-guided UGV navigation. UDAV draws multiple stochastic trajectory predictions, selects their medoid as a self-consistent nominal route, and estimates predictive uncertainty from their spatial dispersion. When the maximum uncertainty across interior waypoints exceeds a threshold, UDAV invokes a reconsideration stage; otherwise, it returns the medoid directly. We evaluate UDAV on 400 held-out trajectory queries from two UAV flights. Stochastic medoid selection reduces the mean average displacement error (ADE) from 147.4 pixels for a deterministic prediction to 115.9 pixels. The complete planner achieves a mean ADE of 110.4 pixels, a 25.1% reduction relative to deterministic planning, while producing valid trajectories for all queries. UDAV also yields the lowest 90th- and 95th-percentile errors among all evaluated configurations, including a higher-budget K=10 consensus baseline. Relative to the K=5 medoid, UDAV reduces these errors from 225.3 and 326.0 pixels to 199.0 and 290.8 pixels, respectively. These results demonstrate that stochastic VLM predictions provide both a stronger nominal route and an actionable uncertainty signal for selectively mitigating large planning errors.

</details>


### [180] [Geometry vs Structure: Graph-Based Diagnostics for LiDAR Point-Cloud Simulation Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16378)
*Ghazal Farhani,Taufiq Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图分析的方法来评估仿真LiDAR点云与真实扫描之间的结构相似性，通过图谱和社区检测分析更准确地刻画仿真数据的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶和ADAS系统大量依赖仿真环境进行传感器验证，但评估仿真点云（特别是3D LiDAR点云）与真实数据的一致性面临挑战，传统几何度量不易捕捉结构性差异。

Method: 以真实和仿真点云构建图结构，采用Louvain社区检测方法分割空间子结构，再基于质心匹配对应社区。针对社区对比，提出基于Weyl不等式的有界图谱指标$r_λ$，并以密度感知Chamfer距离（CDC）为几何对比基线。通过受控扰动实验分析各指标对刚体变化、噪声及结构变形的敏感性。

Result: 在50对真实与仿真LiDAR数据（含超千个社区，涵盖车辆、植被、树木和建筑物墙体等类别）上实验，$r_λ$展现了对位姿变换和噪声的鲁棒性、对结构变化的敏感性。结果表明结构和几何指标能互补反映仿真点云的真实性。

Conclusion: 提出的图谱分析方法能为数字孪生仿真点云的质量验证提供独立且补充的结构视角，对于ADAS与自动驾驶系统的数字孪生验证具有重要价值。

Abstract: Digital twins provide a scalable and cost-effective complement to real-world testing for validating autonomous-driving and advanced driver-assistance system (ADAS) sensor pipelines. However, quantifying their fidelity remains challenging, particularly for 3D LiDAR point clouds, where conventional geometric metrics may overlook important structural discrepancies. We present a graph-based framework for evaluating the structural fidelity of simulated LiDAR point clouds against real-world scans. While scan-level metrics such as Chamfer distance capture point-wise geometric similarity, they do not explicitly represent connectivity, topology, or object-level organization. Our framework constructs graphs from real and simulated point clouds, applies Louvain community detection to identify spatially coherent subgraphs, and matches corresponding communities using centroid proximity. For each matched pair, we compute $r_λ$, a bounded graph-spectral metric motivated by Weyl's inequality, and compare it with density-aware Chamfer distance (CDC) as a geometric baseline. Controlled perturbation experiments demonstrate that $r_λ$ is invariant to rigid transformations and robust to sensor noise while remaining sensitive to structural deformation. We evaluate the framework on 50 paired real and simulated LiDAR scans acquired using a Velodyne VLP-32C sensor and CARLA, respectively. The dataset contains more than 1,000 matched communities across four representative classes: vehicles, vegetation, trees, and building walls. The results show that geometric and structural measures capture complementary aspects of simulation fidelity, supporting graph-spectral analysis as an additional diagnostic layer for validating digital twins in ADAS and autonomous-driving applications.

</details>


### [181] [Collision-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body Control under Imperfect Tracking Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16405)
*Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Ashish Malik,Pranay Dugar,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器人与环境交叉注意力层（RECAL），能够赋予现有的全身控制器以环境感知能力，实现机器人在追踪目标时更有效地避免碰撞。RECAL兼顾目标追踪与碰撞规避，支持多种场景，包括携带物体和多模式运动。实验结果显示，该方法在模拟和实物机器人验证中均优于现有几何感知方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人形机器人全身控制器通常无法感知环境几何信息，容易因感知、规划或远程操控误差导致碰撞。因此，亟需一种方法提升控制器对场景几何的理解，实现更安全的动作执行。

Method: 提出RECAL（Robot–Environment Cross-Attention Layer）：将机器人、携带物体和环境建模为点云，并采用交叉注意力机制捕捉机器人/物体点与环境点之间的关系，生成几何感知控制特征。该机制可作为外部模块包装于现有“盲”WBC控制器，实现动态的目标追踪与碰撞规避权衡，并支持对持有物体的防碰撞。

Result: 在多种仿真场景（固定手臂/自适应手臂行走、携带物体、站立操作）中，RECAL在避免碰撞的同时，保持了较好的追踪性能，并优于其他已知的几何感知控制方法。此外，方法在Digit V3人形机器人实物上也取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: RECAL方法能够有效地将环境几何信息融入现有全身控制器，在稳定追踪目标的同时显著提升了碰撞规避能力，具有较强的普适性与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots often execute motion commands through whole-body controllers (WBCs) that track targets while maintaining balance and stability. However, most WBCs are blind to scene geometry, which can lead to collisions from imperfect target motions that are geometrically unsafe due to perception, planning, or teleoperation errors. We propose RECAL, a Robot--Environment Cross-Attention Layer that wraps a blind WBC to trade off target tracking against collision avoidance using external scene geometry. RECAL supports collision-aware tracking of floating-base and end-effector commands, including collision avoidance for held objects. It represents the robot, held objects, and environment as point clouds, using cross-attention between robot/object points and the environment to produce geometry-aware control features. In simulation, RECAL improves collision avoidance while preserving target-tracking performance across frozen-arm and adaptive-arm locomotion, object-carrying, and standing-manipulation scenarios relative to alternative geometry-aware WBC architectures. We further demonstrate the controller on a real Digit V3 humanoid robot.

</details>


### [182] [XRoboToolKit-T: Teleoperation with High Stability and Precision with Tactile Sensing for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16437)
*Xiwen Dengxiong,Xueting Wang,Ke Jing,Rui Li,Yunbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合了高频、稳定触觉反馈的机器人遥操作系统（XRoboToolKit-T），显著提升了在复杂接触操作任务中的数据采集效率与操作稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据收集解决方案在需要丰富接触的操作任务中，常常无法获得高质量、高频率的触觉反馈，限制了实际应用效果。

Method: 作者设计了XRoboToolKit-T遥操作系统，核心为触觉驱动的辅助控制架构。系统包括：1）利用力分布和伪剪切力推断的stabilizer模块，实现实时触觉辅助；2）结合视觉-语言-动作模型预测和优化操作的refiner模块。系统可处理包括橡胶移液管抓取、医用注射器插入等复杂操作任务。

Result: 在复杂的接触操作任务中，系统展现了优越的操作稳定性和更高的数据采集效率，相比无触觉辅助的主流遥操作，表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 结合高频稳定触觉反馈与多模态建模，XRoboToolKit-T优化了机器人遥操作的数据收集流程与操作效果，提升了复杂环境下的实际可用性。

Abstract: Collecting high-quality robot data for contact-rich manipulation tasks is essential for enabling robots to acquire real-world skills. However, existing data collection solutions often lack the capability to obtain stable and high-frequency tactile feedback, limiting their effectiveness in contact-rich manipulation scenarios. In this work, we propose a versatile teleoperation system with tactile-driven assistance to enable high-frequency and stable contact-rich manipulation. The proposed XRoboToolKit-T teleoperation system incorporates a tactile-informed force control architecture, designed to ensure both stable and precise force control in contact-rich manipulation during teleoperation. The stabilizer haptic module rapidly analyzes the normal force distribution and infers pseudo shear force, enabling real-time tactile-based assistance during manipulation. The refiner haptic module integrates a vision-language-action model to predict and refine manipulation actions based on tactile sensing data and task descriptions. We apply the proposed teleoperation system to challenging contact-rich manipulation tasks, including grasping a deformable rubber pipette for liquid transfer and inserting a medical syringe into a vascular training pad, to demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile-informed force control. Furthermore, the system achieves higher data collection efficiency and improved manipulation stability compared to state-of-the-art teleoperation without tactile assistance.

</details>


### [183] [The Neverwhere Visual Parkour Benchmark Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16443)
*Ziyu Chen,Henghui Bao,Haoran Chang,Alan Yu,Ran Choi,Kai McClennen,Gio Huh,Kevin Yang,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Yajvan Ravan,John J. Leonard,Xiaolong Wang,Phillip Isola,Ge Yang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Neverwhere Benchmark Suite，这是一组包含60多个使用3D高斯散射重建的城市室内外场景，用于高精度评测视觉运动控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉运动控制器能力提升，其在泊接真实环境表现的评估难度加大，亟需真实感强且可重复的仿真测试平台。

Method: 作者构建了基于3D Gaussian Splatting技术的高拟真城市场景集合，支持闭环模拟测试，并提供了集成工具。同时实验分析了仅依赖此类数据训练的策略通用性。

Result: 实验显示，仅用3D高斯重建数据训练的模型在新场景下表现有限，强调了多样化训练数据的重要性。

Conclusion: Neverwhere基准促进了大规模、标准化的机器人视觉运动控制器评测，并提醒社区重视数据多样性以保障模型可迁移性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art visual locomotion controllers are increasingly capable at handling complex visual environments, making evaluating their real-world performance before deployment increasingly difficult. This work intends to narrow this train/evaluation gap by developing a collection of hyper-photo-realistic, closed-loop evaluation environments - The Neverwhere Benchmark Suite - comprised of over sixty 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions of urban indoor and outdoor scenes. Our goal is to encourage large-scale and reproducible robot evaluation by making it easier to create and integrate Gaussian splats-based reconstructions into simulated continuous testing setups. We also underscore the potential pitfalls of relying exclusively on 3D Gaussian-generated data for training, by providing policy checkpoints trained over multiple Neverwhere scenes and their performance when evaluated in novel scenes. Our analysis illustrates the necessity of sourcing diverse data to ensure performance. Code and data are available on the project page: https://ziyc.github.io/neverwhere-bench/.

</details>


### [184] [Dense to MoE Adaptation for Compact Vision Language Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16503)
*Muchun Niu,Shuang Chen,Yuzhou Wu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效精简视觉语言动作（VLA）策略模型参数的方法AdaDE，将部分稠密前馈神经网络转为专家混合（MoE）结构，并动态关闭低用量专家，实现模型大幅瘦身且性能几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略模型参数规模日益增长，导致在资源受限的机器人上部署困难。研究的核心目标是压缩模型参数数量，同时保持下游任务性能不受较大影响。

Method: 提出AdaDE方法，将选定稠密前馈层转为MoE层，初始化时保持原函数，训练时通过路由器统计信息动态生成并更新专家保留掩码，分阶段训练并加入专家保护机制，防止早停塌陷，无需单独恢复步骤。

Result: 在关闭40% LLM参数的情况下，AdaDE在LIBERO任务上仍可保持95.1%的平均成功率，在RobotWin2.0全部50个任务上保持42.0%的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 将稠密结构适配为MoE并动态调整专家激活状态，是在不显著损失性能的情况下有效减少VLA模型体量的可行途径。

Abstract: Vision language action (VLA) policies continue to grow in parameter count, making deployment on resource-constrained robot platforms difficult. The central goal is to reduce the number of LLM-side parameters retained in the deployed policy while preserving downstream task performance. Our approach, AdaDE, adapts selected dense feed forward blocks into mixture of experts (MoE) layers and derives expert retention masks from router statistics during fine tuning. The Dense2MoE conversion preserves the original dense FFN function at initialization, so expert deactivation can start without a separate recovery stage. Instead of using a fixed shutdown rule, expert masks are updated dynamically from router usage statistics, with staged training and expert protection to avoid early collapse. With 40% of the LLM parameters deactivated, AdaDE retains 95.1% average success in LIBERO and 42.0% average success across all 50 RobotWin2.0 tasks. These results suggest that dense to MoE adaptation with dynamic expert deactivation is a practical direction for reducing active VLA model size without severe performance loss.

</details>


### [185] [UniDex-ViTac: Learning Unified Visuo-Tactile Dexterous Manipulation Policy from Human Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16504)
*Hyesung Lee,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniDex-ViTac通过人类视频引导的仿真，为机器人学习灵巧操作生成带有触觉观测的数据，实现了无需真人演示即可提升机器人操控成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频包含丰富的灵巧操作演示，但缺乏机器人可执行的动作和触觉信息，难以直接用于机器人训练。现有方法要么无法充分利用人类演示，要么高度依赖真实机器人采集的数据，局限了泛化性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出UniDex-ViTac框架：利用人类视频指导仿真，自动生成带有指端触觉标注的机器人演示；通过对象专属的强化学习专家，将人类操作迁移到机器人平台，收集带触觉观测的仿真轨迹；最后用这些数据训练结合点云、运动感知和二值触觉信号的通用Transformer策略（ACT方法），无需在部署时依赖人类参考或对象身份与姿势信息。

Result: 在10类对象50段人类演示下，采集1万条仿真轨迹训练策略。点云+触觉配置在仿真成功率为68.3%，高于点云-only基线（55.5%）；现实机器人实验，触觉增强版在11类对象110次测试中成功率66.4%，比基线提升11.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过人类视频引导仿真，联合视觉与触觉信息，可以仅用模拟交互训练出性能更优且实际可部署的统一灵巧操作策略，减少了对真实机器人示范和现场微调的需求。

Abstract: Human videos provide demonstrations of dexterous manipulation but lack robot-executable actions and tactile measurements. We present UniDex-ViTac, a framework that uses human-video-guided simulation to generate robot demonstrations paired with fingertip contact observations for training a deployable visuo-tactile policy. Object-specific residual reinforcement learning specialists adapt annotated human-object interaction references to a robotic arm-hand system. Their successful rollouts pair final robot action targets with robot-side fingertip contact observations. From 50 human demonstrations across ten objects, we collect 10,000 simulated trajectories to train a single Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) based generalist. The policy combines point clouds, proprioception, and four binary contact signals encoded through fingertip labels and a separate token, without requiring human references or privileged object identity and pose at deployment. The contact-augmented configuration achieves 68.3% macro-average success in simulation, compared with 55.5% for the point-cloud-only baseline.
